| `repo-island-models.js`, `repo-island.js` | Repo Island off the south rim, its middle a bridge's span and its own radius out (`spot`, which throws if another islet comes within 8 m): a round voxel island 31.5 m across at the south terrace's height, its edge a stone lip a kerb's step over its top (`LIP`, 1.4 m wide: two staggered rows of flags in grout, a coping and a wall of coursed stones on a plinth), its paths cobbled in staggered rows between kerbstones and its plaza laid in rings of flags round Core (`pavement`), and under it, in place of a rock keel, a crystal cave's ceiling turned to the sky (`crystals()`, `CRYSTAL`: a lumpy dark rock matrix deepest under the middle, hidden under a carpet of 1,800 short see-through crystals (a little less clear than the clusters, `carpetGlass`) packed on a sunflower's spiral so their roots touch (`carpet`), and 150 clusters of slender see-through (`glass`) amethyst quartz on another, each a lead crystal and four to eight shorter ones leaning off it from one root, every crystal six unequal prism faces under an off-axis point, zoned deep at its root to pale at its point in one of three hues between pink and blue; the middle's longest and thickest, all growing mostly down, so the ceiling hangs as one rounded mass lowest under Core; each cluster stands under the totems nearer it than any other; never solid, the walking shell a thin slab; WebGL only, as the totems are), a stone bridge to the lip from the top of the south stairs under the Oogatron (flagged deck ending on the lip's round edge, stepped arch, parapets, pillars and four lanterns), and a carved totem for every repository in `repo-island-data.js`. The ground and its paths are the island's own moss and cobble (`GROUND`), never the main island's grass and dirt. `plan()` lays it out once a page from the data: Bitcoin Core in a paved plaza, the rest of Core's section ringed round it, a ring path, then each other section a wedge between spoke paths, clockwise from north in `SECTIONS` order, each as wide as its totems fill out toward the lip alike (sized by the ground they need, then re-sized over a few packing passes), packed close (`packWedge`: most important first, each in the innermost free place nearest the wedge's middle, a 0.3 m gap between reaches), so the island rises toward Core, every totem facing out toward the lip, out to a gap inside it, and the wedge's carved sign laid flat in the lip at its middle, read from the lip looking in and never in anyone's way; it throws if two reaches meet, one stands on a path or a wedge has no room. Totems are merged into chunks of up to four (`CHUNK`), each carved at three details (`chunkSteps(k, tier)`: `near`, `far`, `distant`), its distant mesh carrying the chunk's collision, runes and crest eyes. The hub calls `turn()` before syncing its solids and, when it turned, `carry(position, feet)` for the Ooga it drives, the rest of the crew and the visitor's gorilla, which turns anyone on or over the island with it; a frame turning more than `MAX_TURN` (the page was away) carries nobody.
